<div class="component-spotlight-content__inner">
<header class="component-spotlight-content__header">
<div class="component-spotlight-content__title">Music &amp; Entertainment</div>
</header>
<div class="component-spotlight-content__content">
<div class="clearfix text-formatted field field--name-field-content field--type-text-long field--label-hidden field__item"><p>If cities had soundtracks, Nashville’s would be like no others. It would be a mix of music’s past, present and future with cuts of country, bluegrass, rock, pop, Americana, gospel, classical, jazz and blues, all blending and overlapping in perfect harmony. </p>
